#### IMPORTANT INFORMATION:

- You can bid by mail, phone, fax, email or via our website - see contact details below.

- For enquiries on any lots, contact our office as above.

- Lots are listed on a simplified basis unless otherwise stated.

- Stamps illustrated in colour may not be exactly true to shade.

- Bids under 2/3rds of estimate are generally not considered.

- NZ Bidders: GST of 15% will be added to successful bids.

**EXCHANGE RATES** at time of printing (subject to change) NZ Dollar equals UK 51p, USA 72c, 
Australia 96c, Canada 90c, Euro 62c, Japan ¥82, Hong Kong $5.65, Singapore 97c.

## MOWBRAY COLLECTABLES

Private Bag 63000, Wellington 6140, New Zealand Phone +64 6 364 8270,Fax +64 6 364 8252 Email auctions@mowbrays.nz, website www.mowbrays.co.nz

## CONDITIONS OF SALE

1. We shall accept bids offered by post, e-mail, telephone, via our website www.mowbrays.co.nz, or facsimile.

2. The highest bidder shall be the purchaser (subject to reserve, which is normally 65%-80% of estimate). If two bids are received for identical amounts on any lot,the first bid received shall be awarded the lot.

3. Bids shall advance in the following increments: Up Over Bids shall be accepted only in these advances; odd an increments.

$100 $5 increments $100 10% increments, or at our discretion. unts if offered will be reduced to the nearest acceptable

4. Bids will be used to their maximum value only as competitive bids require. Where two or more identical lots are listed, we reserve the right to transfer a bid from one lot to another if this is in the bidder's interests.

5. We reserve the right to refuse any bid.

6. "Buy" bids will not be accepted (in the event of two such bids on a single lot, an impossible situation would exist).

7. We will accept alternative bids for one of several lots if the bidder's intention is made clear.

8. We reserve the right to sell more than one lot where multiple lots, identical to the description, are available. This shall not apply to an illustrated lot.

9. By placing bids in the auction, the bidder is entering into a binding contract to purchase all lots he is awarded.

10. Maximum purchase value may be nominated by bidders. This figure must be noted in the bid form - when the limit has been reached all further bids will be cancelled.

11. Payment: Bidders will receive an invoice after the auction. Payment should be forwarded within 7 days of receipt of invoice and may be made by credit or debit card - or: within NZ - deposit to our bank account; Australia - personal cheque for AUD equivalent amount, or deposit to our NAB account; USA/Canada - personal check for USD/CAD equivalent amount; UK - personal cheque in STG equivalent amount, or deposit to our Lloyds account.

12. Buyers from outside New Zealand undertake that neither they nor an associated person will cause the purchased goods to be re-imported into New Zealand in substantially the same condition in which they were exported. This means that GST will not be charged on their purchases.

13. Delivery shall be made to bidders upon receipt of payment. Postage and packing are at purchaser's expense and unless otherwise requested, dispatches will be mailed: within New Zealand by ordinary mail; overseas by airmail; all dispatches valued over $1,000 by registered mail or courier.

14. Lots are listed on a simplified basis, unless otherwise stated.

15. Guarantee: Each lot is guaranteed to be genuine and as described. Descriptions of condition are opinion, and we recognise that opinions vary and that errors occur. Should purchasers suspect a lot is not as described, they should notify the Auctioneer within 7 days of receipt of the item. A replacement, refund or credit will be arranged. The following lots are not returnable:

(a) Items which were illustrated in the catalogue are not returnable for matters of centering, postmarks, perf defects (ie. visible faults).

(b) Stamps which have been immersed in water or subjected to any chemical process subsequent to the sale. If necessary, the opinion of a mutually acceptable authority shall be sought, and such opinion shall be final.

16. Viewing lots: lots may be viewed by appointment at the offices of Mowbray Collectables in Otaki. Alternatively, arrangements may be made for items to be photocopied or scanned (within reason).

17. Placing a bid in this Auction shall constitute acceptance of the foregoing conditions.

18. Prices Realised are sent to all bidders after each sale and is available to view on our website after the sale.

ABBREVIATIONS USED IN THIS CATALOGUE:

| * | - MUH (mint, not hinged) | H/H | - heavy hinged | pmk | - postmark |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| / | - mint &amp; used | h/s | - handstamp | PPC | - picture postcard |
| ACS | - Auckland City Stamps (NZ) | LH | - light hinged | PU | - postally used |
| b/s | - backstamp | LJ | - Len Jury | R | - revenue |
| BW | - Brusden White | M | - mint, previously hinged | r/s | - rubber stamp |
| C | - cover/entire/postcard | MNG | - mint no gum | S | - specimen |
| cds | - circular date stamp | m/s | - miniature sheet | seln | - selection |
| CP | - Campbell Paterson (NZ) | or manuscript postmark |  | SG | - Stanley Gibbons (UK) |
| CTO | - cancelled to order | o/c | - off centre | s/l | - straight line |
| FDC | - first day cover | OG | - original gum | "GU" and "FU" refer to the postmark |  |
| FU | - fine used (light pmk) | P | - proof/reprint |  |  |
| GU | - good used | p/c | - postcard |  |  |

otherwise nice condition.

sealed" label & detailed "Caution" label (ptg. no. "2350").  M/s "Comp. Regd. 10d to pay" & blue
pencil regn. lines added.  Some subsequent bank notation.  Slightly cut down & front and back

---

465 C 1900 Portugal 10R p/c sent from Lourenco Marques (in modern Mozambique,) to NZ.  Added $150
Lourence Marques 2½R pair & 5R adhesives canc cds "21 MAI 09" (should be year "00"). 
Hexagonal "5" postage due marking, Durban transit cds 28 MY 00 & Sydney cds 11 JY 00.  NZ 1d 
due marking & 1d PD canc Auckland cds 19 JL 00.  Several faults (fragile card). Highly unusual 
origin for mail to NZ.
466 C 1905 GB PPC franked ½d EVII canc, Nottingham 16 NO 05, to NZ, charged postage due, then re-$150
addressed (twice) to NSW, Australia, NZ 1d postage due marking, with 1d Postage Due canc 
Dunedin cds 26 DE 05.  Also Australia 1d Postage Due canc. fine Bulyeroi/NSW cds 13 JA 06.  
Rare example of postage due paid in 2 diff countries on the same item.
467 C 1906 cover Argentina to NZ, franked 6c stamp canc. Bahia Blanca cds (date unclear).  "T" postage $150
due marking with m/s "15". Later NZ "TO PAY 3d...."due marking, 1d &2d Postage Due stamps 
canc Kirikiriroa 28 MR 06 & 2nd strike (with Hamilton cds) on back.  Unusual pictorial cover with art 
nouveau design, headed "Tarjeta Postal" (Post card).  Circular cutout at upper right to display 
stamp on enclosed PPC (which is no longer present).  Despite this private initiative, the PPC had to 
be sent at the higher envelope rate.  Some faults, but rare.
468 C 1942 long GB printed OHMS cover (vert fold) to NZ with large pictorial cachet (NZ Map) "The $80
Dominion of New Zealand". Franked by very scarce, blue war-time cds "OFFICIAL PAID/H.C. FOR 
NZ" 11 JAN 1942 (few examples known).
469 C 1950 air cover South Africa to NZ franked 1/-, 3d pictorials canc. Jo'burg cds 15.VII.50. m/S "T6d" $35
then NZ "TO PAY/DOUBLE DEFICIENT POSTAGE" h/s plus m/s "6d".  NZ GVI 6d addded & canc 
Wellington cds 27 JL 50.  Fine.
470 C 1892 registered cover to GB "via Frisco" (m/s), franked 6d FSF (very late use) & ½d Newspaper $125
dull rose (late use of thin paper, star wmk variety), canc Christchurch A type cds 20 JA 92 & 
separate (illegible) barred obliterator.  Two receipt b/s. The postage is puzzling: at this time, basic 
letter rate postage to GB was 2½d per ½ ounce, regn was 6d extra.  Possibly 6d mistaken for 8d 
SSF?  Fine and attractive.
471 C 1893 cover (unsealed) to USA, franked ½d newspaper canc fine AUCKLAND F-type d/s 25 FE 93. $75
Good condition.
472 C 1894 1d blue p/c to photographic publishers in London, GB, with added ½d Newspaper adhesive, $40
canc fine NELSON F-type d/s 5 JE 94.  Wellington A-type transit cds.  Light ageing, otherwise fine.
473 C 1894 1d QV blue p/c to USA, with added ½d Newspaper adhesive, canc 2 fine CHRISTCHURCH B-$40
type cds 26 DE 94.  Two US transit/receipt cds.  Fine.
474 C 1894 1d QV blue p/c to GB with added ½d Newspaper adhesive, canc fine Auckland F-type d/s 7 $45
SP 94.  Message re supply from NZ of "fossils, ferns, shells, minerals & bird skins, eggs, etc".  Fine.
475 C 1895 cover to GB franked ½d Newspaper & damaged 2d SSF, canc 2 strikes (1 fair-good, 1 poor) $35
of TE PUKE A-type cds 5 AU 95, with 3 fine b/s incl Tauranga.  Nice condition.
476 C 1901 neat cover to Novgorod, Russia, franked 1½d Boer War p11 & pair ½d Mt Cook p11, canc 2 $140
Morrinsville A-type cds 24 JUL 01, m/s "Via Frisco".  B/s Auckland cds & 3 Russian cds.  Unusual 
destination.  Nice condition.
477 C 1903 registered cover to Natal, South Africa, franked 1st Pictorials 3d Huia, 1½d Boer War, ½d Mt $90
Cook (all p11), canc 2 Christchurch G-type cds 28 JL 03, light boxed regn h/s.  Two b/s.  Small 
edge tear (into 1½d), otherwise fine.
478 C 1908 registered cover to GB (one corner reduced). Unusual frianking of horiz. pair 1½d Boer War $80
p11, canc TIMARU A type cds 8 SP 08, m/s "A.R.".  Two transit/receipt b/s.  The rate on this should 
have been 6½d (1d postage + 3d regn + 2½d advice of delivey).  No sign of any other stamp.
479 C 1921 registered cover to "Bangkok, Siam, Indo-Chinese Peninsula, with philatelic franking of 1st $150
pictorials 2½d Wakatipu, 1½d Boer War p14 (infrequently seen on cover), 2d, 1d & 2 diff ½d (total 
8d) canc Christchurch cds 14 JA 21.  Three transit b/s.  Opened 3 sides. Six- colour franking!
480 1902 New Zealand Ocean Mail Timetables, 2½ x 3¾ inches booklet printed by John Mackay $50
Government Printer (5,200/1/02-897]) 22 pages with postal rates & sailing dates to Vancouver, 
London, France etc.
481 C 1870 cover, 3d FFQ (perf), p12½ with Napier duplex JU 30/70 to Miss Earp, Boscobel Farm, $150
Porirua, Wellington (with contents), endorsed "per Taranaki" & Wellington/AU4/70 b/s.
482 C 1873 printed "On Public Service Only" stampless long cover, printed "Otago Gold Fields" at lower $200
left crossed out & m/s "[??] School, Caversham, 28/1/73" over top, illegible blue pencilled m/s frank,
Dunedin "O" duplex pmk JA 30 73, to local address.  Light vert fold & light overall soiling.  2 pieces
of tape on back.
483 C 1874 cover, 2d FFQ (blue), p12½, with dumb obliterator of Tutaenui, to Miss Earp, Boscobel, $250
Porirua Road, Wellington, with contents (a love letter), TUTAENUI/JU 10/68 & WELLINGTON/JY 
14/68 b/ss.  Also 2 newspaper clippings re Boscobel.
484 C 1876 Wellington A class 31 MR 76 duplex on 2d FSF on cover with "WI1", temporary A type cds of $200
Otaki AP? 76.  Letter addressed to Otaki.  Very few known.
